Volume - 160 BENGALURU, MONDAY, 29, DECEMBER, 2025 (PUSHYA , 08, SHAKAVARSHA, 1947) Issue 249

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RC-CFS0ADMN/372/2025 Karnataka Government Secretariat
E-1878060 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru-560 001,
Date:26-12-2025.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Where as the draft of the Notification No: RC-CFS0ADMN/372/2025 Dated:
05-11-2025 regarding altering the limits of 63-Timmalapura village of Hadagali
Taluk and Vijayanagar District and constituting a new revenue village called Rajvala
and Sub village Rajvala plot was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette
Dated: 12-11-2025 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Vijayanagar district.
And where as, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 14-11-2025 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village 63-Timmalapura of Hadagali taluk in Vijayanagar District to form a new
revenue village called as Rajvala of Hadagali taluk Vijayanagar District along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Where as the draft of the Notification No. RC-CC0RVN/12/2025,
Dated: 23-09-2025 regarding altering the limits of Kokatanur village of Athani
Taluk in Belagavi District and constituting a new revenue sub village called as Kuri
Daddi Balesab Vasati & Horatti subvillage was published as required by section 6 of
the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the
Karnataka Gazette Dated: 08-10-2025 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Belagavi district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:13-10-2025. And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Kuri Daddi village of Athani Taluk in
Belagavi District convert the same to form hamlet within the boundary of Kokatanur
revenue village and same will here in after be named as called Kuri Daddi of
Kokatanur village in Athani Taluk of Belagavi District.

SCHEDULE-2
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Balesab Vasati village of Athani Taluk in
Belagavi District convert the same to form hamlet within the boundary of
Kokatanur revenue village and same will here in after be named as called Balesab
Vasati of Kokatanur village in Athani Taluk of Belagavi District.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No.:RC-CFS/287/2025
Dated 12-11-2025 regarding altering the limits of Shingasandra village of
Harohalli Taluk and Bangalore South District and constituting a new revenue village
called Hosa Badavane and Hale Badavane was published as required by section 6 of

the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the
Karnataka Gazette Dated 14.11.2025 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated 14.11.2025; And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Hosa Badavane village of Harohalli
Taluk of Bangalore South District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within
the boundary of Shingasandra Revenue village and same will herein after be named
as Hosa Badavane of Shingasandra village in Harohalli Taluk of Bangalore South
District.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RC-CFS/216/2025 Dated 07-11-2025 regarding
altering the limits of Shivasandra village of Gubbi Taluk and Tumakuru District and constituting
a new revenue village called Shivasandra Badavane was published as required by section 6 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette Dated
13-11-2025 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated 14-11-2025.
And whereas, objections or suggestions have not been received by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land Revenue Act,
1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here by makes the following
notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the table shall be
declared as habitation area of Shivasandra Badavane village of Gubbi Taluk of Tumakuru District
convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary of Shivasandra Revenue village
and same will herein after be named as Shivasandra Badavane of Shivasandra village in Gubbi
Taluk of Tumakuru District.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RC-CFS0ADMN/746/2025-RC-REVENUE
COMMISSIONERATE Dated 20.09.2025 regarding altering the limits of Kalmane village of
Shikaripura Taluk and Shivamogga District and constituting a new revenue village called
Chowdihalli was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette Dated 11.11.2025 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated 16.11.2025.
And whereas, objections or suggestions have not been received by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land Revenue Act,
1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here by makes the following
notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the table shall be
declared as habitation area of Chowdihalli village of Shikaripura Taluk of Shivamogga District
convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary of Kalmane Revenue village and
same will herein after be named as Chowdihalli of Kalmane village in Shikaripura Taluk of
Shivamogga District.

thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette
addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Tumakuru district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 21-06-2024; And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Jiddegere of Kunigal Taluk and Tumakuru District to form a new revenue
village called as Shiddlanahatti of Kunigal Taluk in Tumakuru District along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RC-CFS0ADMN/778/2025 Dated 20.09.2025
regarding altering the limits of Baluru village of Shikaripura Taluk and Shivamogga District and
constituting a new revenue village called Ashraya Badavane was published as required by section 6
of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette
Dated 11.11.2025 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated 18.11.2025.
And whereas, objections or suggestions have not been received by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land Revenue Act,
1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here by makes the following
notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the table shall be
declared as habitation area of Ashraya Badavane village of Shikaripura Taluk of Shivamogga
District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary of Baluru Revenue village
and same will herein after be named as Ashraya Badavane of Baluru village in Shikaripura Taluk
of Shivamogga District.

The proposal specified above shall come into effect from such date as the State Government
may specify by Notification proposed to be issued under section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964).

regarding altering the limits of K. Benchamatti village of Kushtagi Taluk and Koppal District and
constituting a new revenue village called New K.Benchamatti was published as required by section
6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette
Dated 11-11-2025 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated 19-11-2025.
And whereas, objections or suggestions have not been received by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land Revenue Act,
1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here by makes the following
notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the table shall be
declared as habitation area of New K.Benchamatti village of Kushtagi Taluk of Koppal District
convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary of K. Benchamatti Revenue village
and same will herein after be named as New K.Benchamatti of K. Benchamatti village in Kushtagi
Taluk of Koppal District.

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RC-CFS0ADMN/433/2025 Dated 04-09-2025
regarding altering the limits of Dhanapur village of Gangavathi Taluk and Koppal District and
constituting a new revenue village called Ramapura was published as required by section 6 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette Dated
03-10-2025 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated 18-11-2025.
And whereas, objections or suggestions have not been received by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land Revenue Act,
1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here by makes the following
notification, namely:-

declared as habitation area of Ramapura village of Gangavathi Taluk of Koppal District convert
the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary of Dhanapur Revenue village and same
will herein after be named as Ramapura of Dhanapur village in Gangavathi Taluk of Koppal
District.
